- W11 (the pipe client). `ConnectError` keeps its four variants, and a server that fails the user and elevation check is `Refused` (design part 9.6). The client opens the pipe with `SECURITY_IDENTIFICATION`, so a squatting server cannot impersonate it.
- W12 (the share credential's value). A share credential's value is two lines, a user name and a password, both non-empty, split by `core::ipc::share_credential`, and `secret_set` refuses another shape with `<name> could not be stored: the value must be a user name and a password on two lines` (design part 9.4).
- W13 (Credential Manager). Every secret is a generic credential with the target `lazymkv:` and the secret name (so `lazymkv:share:\\server\share`, as design part 6.1 fixes), the user name `lazymkv`, the value's UTF-8 bytes as its blob and `CRED_PERSIST_LOCAL_MACHINE`. `get` is None on any error, so over SSH a stored secret reads as unset, `set`'s error is `Credential Manager refused the entry: <error>`, and S77 stays: no listing.
- W14 (the share connector). The first attempt probes the share root `\\server\share\` (or a drive root `X:\`), not the configured folder, so a missing `engine` folder is not an unreachable share, and the stored secret for a root is looked up as `share:` and the share as written, Windows ignoring case (design part 6.1, P7). A credential Windows refused is not tried again until its value changes, so a wrong stored password cannot lock the owner's NAS account, and `access` reports how the last `reach` of that share got in.
- W15 (engine folders). Before CheckSpace's first write and in the self-test, the engine creates a missing `staging_root` or `tuning_root` with `fs::create_dir` when its parent exists, never deeper and never a library root (design part 6.2). The self-test's root check asks `reach` before its probe (spec 13: "each root reachable"). Amended at review round 1 of pull request 3 (R25, R26, R36), below.
- W16 (`started`). The rip step records `host.now_ms()` as `TitleRecord::rip_started_ms` in its posted record edits before `tools::rip` runs, and VerifyFile writes `rip-summary.json`'s `started` from it with `host.local_time`, so a restart between the two loses nothing (R23.4, R23.7). A record without the field writes no `started`.
- W17 (the writable test). `WinHost::user_writable` runs `AccessCheck` with the filtered token: the linked token when the process token's elevation type is Full, else `CreateRestrictedToken(LUA_TOKEN)` at medium integrity, and any failure answers true (amends design part 3.6's filtered token: with no linked token a restricted token stands in; Owner decision 11: no answer recorded by 2026-10-29, so it stands until the owner overturns it).
- W18 (the tool lookup). `find_tool` drops relative and empty `PATH` entries, skips a `PATH` folder or program file and a `resources` folder that `host.user_writable` calls writable, and tries a settings path that is not a file again with `EXE_SUFFIX` appended (design part 3.6, S18). The default install folders come from the registry values `ProgramFilesDir (x86)` and `ProgramFilesDir` under `HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE\SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion`, read unexpanded, not from the `%ProgramFiles(x86)%` and `%ProgramFiles%` variables (amended after the whole-branch review (F1), below). Amended at review round 1 of pull request 3 (V2), below: a `resources` program file that `host.user_writable` calls writable is skipped too, as a `PATH` one is.
- W19 (the start check and Ctrl+C). The console handler is installed before `start`, so a stop event during `start` ends the process at once and the Job Objects end its listing; after `start` returns, a stop event sends a forced shutdown (design part 3.8). Amended at review round 1 of pull request 3 (V4, V8), below.
- W20 (bounded listings). The MakeMKV check at start and the self-test's listing pass `limits.header` (120 s) as their limit, and a listing killed by that limit or by the exit wait (M2) is reported not ok with its own sentence; a job's listing keeps no limit (amends S57 for the checks outside a job; Owner decision 11: no answer recorded by 2026-10-29, so it stands until the owner overturns it).
- W21 (AwaitingShare). The re-check asks `reach`, then `free_bytes`, of the same roots, and for `staging_root` `space::root_ready`, so a free-space error, or a staging root that is not a folder and cannot be made, keeps the job waiting and a retry never cycles through ShareBack (amends S81; Owner decision 11: no answer recorded by 2026-10-29, so it stands until the owner overturns it). Amended at review round 1 of pull request 3 (V11, R26), below.
- W22 (library reads). `library_facts` reads `NotFound` as absent and turns any other error into `DecisionFailed { Unknown }` with `the library folder <path> could not be read: <error>`, so decide's output is unchanged for inputs without errors (R-B1), and Decide asks `reach` of the library root first, as File does (V7) (amends decide's inputs, which are R-B1-sensitive; Owner decision 11: no answer recorded by 2026-10-29, so it stands until the owner overturns it). Amended at review round 1 of pull request 3 (R28), below.
- W23 (unpersisted transitions). `feed` returns `Fed::{Rejected, Persisted, NotPersisted}`, and `step_done` stops feeding a report's later events after `NotPersisted` (RR1-16). An event whose transition was not persisted from a state with no failure row (Planned, RipVerified, an Ejected job's FilePhaseSlot) is kept and fed again on a one-minute timer until it persists (V1).
- W24 (the relay). The engine reads a `command`'s `words` with `lazymkv_dev::forms::read_forms`, the parser `lazymkvctl` uses, puts its built words first and the client's after, so that `Args`' last-wins rule lets an explicit flag win, and runs `EngineConfig.ctl` below normal priority in the engine's working folder, from a thread of its own that reports its end to the owner (design parts 4.4, 9.5). A client that disconnects kills its child, a `shutdown` kills it too, and every refusal is an error reply, so `lazymkvctl` exits 2 (amends S76: `command`'s refusal is retired, and the relay's are `COMMAND_BUSY`, `COMMAND_NO_CTL`, `COMMAND_WRITABLE`, `COMMAND_FORMS`, read_forms' errors, `RETUNE_WAITS`, `no job <id>` (S76), `no_title`, `not_started` and `STOPPING`, design part 9.5's two refusals thus gaining `COMMAND_FORMS`, `COMMAND_WRITABLE` and `no_title`; Owner decision 11: no answer recorded by 2026-10-29, so it stands until the owner overturns it). Amended at review round 1 of pull request 3 (R15), below. Amended by pull request 5 (G2; T5-22): `--evidence` accumulates, so the engine's `--evidence <tuning_root>\recordings=<tuning_root>\dataset` comes first and each client `--evidence` adds a folder after it; the last flag still wins for every single-valued flag.
- W25 (what pull request 3 relays). The relay runs `plan --job` and `decide --job --title` and never adds `--text`, since no dataset copy exists before pull request 5, and refuses `retune --jobs` with `RETUNE_WAITS`; the stale-copy refetch and K15's exit 3 stay pull request 4's, so the child reads the copies the job folder holds (amends design part 9.5's three relayed forms until pull request 5; Owner decision 11: no answer recorded by 2026-10-29, so it stands until the owner overturns it). Closed by pull request 5 (calls 13 and 14; T5-22): `RETUNE_WAITS` is gone, `retune --jobs` is relayed with the tuning trees first (T5-R53), refused with `RETUNE_NO_TUNING_ROOT` when `tuning_root` is unset (T5-R32), and `--text <tuning_root>\dataset` is added to `decide --job --title` once the job's `tuning_copy` is `done` (T5-R14).
- W26 (the binary's services). The Windows binary passes `OfflineTransport` (lookups stay pending), `NoDelivery` (each notification logged undelivered), `StdTimers` and no scripted steps, with `lazymkvctl` beside its own program as `ctl`, `<exe folder>\..\resources` as `resources`, the process `PATH` and standard error as its console (spec 12, design part 3.5).
- W27 (pull request 3's running whole job). The CI `whole_job` runs unchanged on three systems, and the native whole job runs on `windows-latest` and the mini PC through the real start-up sequence, pipe and client, the Job Object runner, the Windows file moves and the real `Host`, `KeepAwake` and a local-folder `ShareConnector`, with scripted drives, a recording ejector, a recorded transport and memory secrets (design part 12.6 item 3). S55 stops every stand-in `makemkvcon` call while a MakeMKV process runs, so off CI that test prints `skipped: a MakeMKV process is running on this machine (S55)` and passes when one does.
- W28 (L7's test). After `create_dir_all` of the final path's folder, File opens that folder with `fs::read_dir` before the rename, and an error there gives L7's message and `FileFailed { Unknown }` (design part 6.5). The folder opened is the title's own, never the library root, so LIB-7 stands.

### Pull request 3: the task-level rulings

Each task of pull request 3's plan recorded these rulings beside its steps; they are named `T3-<task>-<letter>`.

**Task T3-1** (`windows-sys` and the `Cargo.lock` delta)
- T3-1-a. Reading crates.io's application programming interface for a version's licence, publication date, checksum, features and dependencies downloads no crate and changes nothing on either machine, so it ran before the owner's yes; the first download is the `--locked` build against the approved entries (PF-11).

**Task T3-2** (token facts and the engine mutex)
- T3-2-a. The mutex is created with `bInitialOwner` false and never waited on: its existence is the lock (design part 3.3), since a mutex's owning thread takes it again at once and only that thread may release it.
- T3-2-b. An existing mutex owned by Administrators is `EngineRunning`; any other owner that can be read is a `Squatter` named `<domain>\<name>` by `LookupAccountSidW` (the SID string when the SID has no name), and an owner that cannot be read, or a name another kind of object holds, is a squatter named `an account lazymkv cannot read`.
- T3-2-c. `TokenFacts.linked` is true when the token has a linked token, which is closed at once; a `Default` token has none.
- T3-2-d. The token test writes its facts line past the test harness's capture, so a plain `cargo test` shows it in `windows-latest`'s log and over SSH.

**Task T3-3** (the protected data folder)
- T3-3-a. An absent folder is created by `CreateDirectoryW` with the owner `BA` and the protected list in its security attributes, so on a volume that keeps access lists it is protected from its first moment; the owner check and the list set follow the creation too, so a volume that ignores those attributes cannot leave it open. Only the last component is created.
- T3-3-b. A path whose own attributes carry a reparse point (a symbolic link or a junction), or a path that is not a folder, is refused before any change, because `SetNamedSecurityInfoW` follows a link.
- T3-3-c. The owner is checked before the list is set, so a refused folder keeps its list.

**Task T3-4** (the file moves on `MoveFileExW`)
- T3-4-a. `MoveFileExW` with `MOVEFILE_REPLACE_EXISTING` refuses a target any handle holds open, as the test harness's polls, a virus scanner or an indexer may, so the replace tries again on os errors 5, 32 and 33, 10 tries 10 ms apart; the store's state.json retry (RR1-2) stays around it, and the cost is at most 90 ms per write against a target held for good.
- T3-4-b. File's hard-link half state (`one_file`, `half_done` and the two arms of `file` that use them) compiles only off Windows, with the restart test that builds it; on Windows a hard link at the final path is a collision like any other file.

**Task T3-5** (the Job Object runner)
- T3-5-a. The job handle is shared by the child and every `KillHandle` made from it, so a kill works after the child is dropped and the tree is killed at the latest when the last of them is dropped; `run_recorded` holds both only for the run.
- T3-5-b. The suspended child's one thread is found through Toolhelp and resumed with `OpenThread` and `ResumeThread`, because `ChildExt::main_thread_handle` is unstable and the workspace pins a stable toolchain.
- T3-5-c. The helper modes write `\nchild <pid>\n` and `\ngrandchild <pid>\n`, flushed, the leading newline keeping each line apart from libtest's `test ... ` prefix.

**Task T3-6** (`KeepAwake` on the thread execution state)
- T3-6-a. The count lives under the caller's lock, and a change from 0 to 1 or 1 to 0 waits for the thread's reply, so the system state matches the count when `acquire` or `release` returns; a send or a reply that fails is ignored, since the trait gives no error to return.

**Task T3-22** (the `command` relay)
- T3-22-a. `send_waiting` waits until the client's queue holds fewer than `CLIENT_QUEUE - COMMAND_HEADROOM` (192) lines, not 256, so a burst never leaves the queue full for the next broadcast, which S52 would answer by dropping the client; `Clients` counts each client's queued lines for it.
- T3-22-b. The refusals come in this order: `COMMAND_BUSY`, `COMMAND_NO_CTL`, `COMMAND_WRITABLE`, then the words' (`COMMAND_FORMS` for a first word other than `plan`, `decide` or `retune`, read_forms' error, `RETUNE_WAITS` for `--jobs`, `COMMAND_FORMS` without `--job`, `no job <id>` (S76), `no_title`), and last a spawn error (`not_started`); each is an error reply, so `lazymkvctl` exits 2 (RP24). Superseded by pull request 5 (T5-22): `RETUNE_WAITS` is gone; `retune --jobs` is relayed, and in its place in the order comes `RETUNE_NO_TUNING_ROOT` when the engine's settings hold no `tuning_root`.
- T3-22-c. A client's reader thread sends `Msg::Gone` once it closes the client, and the owner kills a command that client runs; a `send_waiting` that finds its client gone kills the child too, so a child that prints nothing still ends when its client leaves.
- T3-22-d. An accepted `shutdown`, and the owner's `shut_down`, kill the running command's child, answer its `command` with `STOPPING` (`the engine is stopping (design part 3.8)`) and close that client, so a client that never reads cannot hold the stop.
- T3-22-e. The owner thread calls `ProcessGuard::spawn`, which only creates the process, so it holds the kill handle from the start, and the relay thread reads, waits and reports (design part 4.4).

**Task T3-23** (the engine binary on Windows and the native whole job)
- T3-23-a. `NativeOptions` gains `processes`: production passes None (`native_guard()`), and the native harness passes its `TestGuard`, whose inner guard is `native_guard()`, so the native job keeps the wait for the recorded lookups (R23.2) and every child still runs under the Job Object runner (W27).
- T3-23-b. The native harness has `protect_data_folder` create its data folder, owned by Administrators with the protected list, and writes its settings back, because a folder the test process makes need not be owned by Administrators (design part 3.5).
- T3-23-c. The native harness's mutex is `Global\` and the pipe's name, `native_exit` gives a test `run_with`'s exit code, and the harness's drop stops a run still going with a forced `shutdown` through the pipe and asserts exit 0.
- T3-23-d. A token that cannot be read, a drive watcher, keep-awake or console handler that cannot start, a pipe error other than a held name and an Err from `start` print `lazymkv-engine: the engine could not start: <error>` and exit 1 (W2); a stop event before `start` returned writes `STOPPED_STARTING` and exits 1 at once (W19).

**Task T3-24** (the desktop task script)
- T3-24-a. The run folder's list is set before the copy, from `O:BA` and `folder_sddl`'s exact string with `DirectorySecurity.SetSecurityDescriptorSddlForm`, so no non-administrator can write the folder while the programs land in it, and a folder already holding a file the script did not put there is refused, since an elevated program loads a library planted beside it (design part 3.5) (amends design part 3.1's order, which lists the copy first; Owner decision 11: no answer recorded by 2026-10-29, so it stands until the owner overturns it).
- T3-24-b. The task's working folder is `C:\lazymkv\run`, so neither the engine nor the relay's child starts in `C:\Windows\System32`.

**Task T3-25** (the mini PC runs)
- T3-25-a. The controller relays each cue by reading the job's log every 10 s over plain SSH, because `wait.sh`'s first report of a running job comes after about 520 s, past a cue's 5-minute wait.
- T3-25-b. The share folder of the SSH and desktop share tests is `\\unas-pro\media\lazymkv-staging\pr3-tests`, under phase 0's write area (Owner decision 9); it stays, empty.
- T3-25-c. The real disc is stopped with `stop --force` once its first title rips: with the offline transport (W26) its job plans with its identity pending, and the disc is a movie disc that is neither acceptance disc of design part 12.7.
- T3-25-d. The arranged share run comes last, after the desktop task, because only the desktop task's engine writes the owner's vault (design part 6.1).
- T3-25-e. The console run names five stand-ins, `java` among them, as the harness does, so no real program on the elevated engine's `PATH` runs.
- T3-25-f. The console run's data folder is created from the elevated SSH session before the first start, its owner set to Administrators if needed, because its `settings.json` must name the stand-ins before the start check runs. Since review round 1 of pull request 3 (V3), the console run's `settings.json`, like the folder, must be owned by Administrators before the first start.
- T3-25-g. `engine-task.ps1` first runs without `-AcceptWritableBuild`, so Owner decision 13's check meets the real build folders, and the switch is passed only after the owner's yes.
- T3-25-h. `platform-ignored.ps1` gets one parse test, as `engine-task.ps1` has, because a parse error in a hidden desktop job would show only after a wait and a disc cue.
- T3-25-i. The console run copies its programs into `C:\lazymkv\dev\bin` and `C:\lazymkv\dev\tools`, owned by Administrators with a protected list (full control for SYSTEM and Administrators, read and execute for Users) set before the copies, because the elevated SSH session runs them.

### Pull request 3: the owner's decisions

The plan's eighteen owner decisions, with the owner's answers; the recommended option was taken wherever there was one.

- 1 (the `Cargo.lock` delta): yes, `windows-sys` 0.61.2 and `windows-link` 0.2.1, both MIT OR Apache-2.0, 43 to 45 packages, approved as read, 2026-10-28
- 2 (the mini PC's console runs): yes: the console run's folders under `C:\lazymkv\dev` (`data`, `staging`, `tuning`, `library\Movies`, `bin`, `tools`), release copies in `dev\bin`, `fake-tool.exe` as the five stand-in tools (`java` among them) in `dev\tools`, owner Administrators and the protected list on `dev\bin` and `dev\tools`, `settings.json` before the first start, and the engine protecting `dev\data` at each start; an existing `dev\data` owned by the owner's account is removed and created again, and the owner takes the disc out of `D:` before the console run, 2026-10-28
- 3 (the scheduled task `lazymkv-engine`): yes, one yes for phase 2: register the on-demand task (no trigger, the owner's account, interactive, highest run level), create `C:\lazymkv\run` with the protected list, and register it again on every later `engine-task.ps1` deploy, 2026-10-28
- 4 (the task's settings): no time limit (`-ExecutionTimeLimit` zero), priority 7 kept, battery conditions off, the console window shown, 2026-10-28
- 5 (the desktop task's data folder): `%ProgramData%\lazymkv`, with no `--data`, which the engine creates and protects at its first start; the script keeps `-Data DIR`, 2026-10-28
- 6 (the desktop-session test runs): yes: `scripts/phase0/platform-ignored.ps1` runs through `job.sh`, registering `lazymkv-phase0` again with `-Force` on each run; the owner at the monitor follows the disc cues relayed in chat; `LAZYMKV_TEST_DRIVE=D:` (upper case), 2026-10-28

- 8 (Credential Manager entries): confirmed: `CRED_PERSIST_LOCAL_MACHINE` stays, and the round-trip test may write and delete a `lazymkv-test-...` entry in the owner's vault in the desktop session (`LAZYMKV_TEST_VAULT` set for the `win::creds` job only), 2026-10-28
- 9 (first writes to the NAS): yes: the desktop runs write under `\\unas-pro\media\lazymkv-staging\engine` and `\\unas-pro\media\lazymkv-tuning\engine`, the real-share tests under `lazymkv-staging\pr3-tests` (left empty), the self-test's probe in the test library's `.lazymkv-partial`, and the real disc's job uses the three share roots, 2026-10-28

- 11 (the rulings that change earlier rulings or the design's letter: W3, W8, W10, W17, W20, W21, W22, W24, W25 and T3-24-a): no answer recorded; the rulings stand until the owner overturns any of them, 2026-10-29
- 12 (account names on the console and in `engine.log`): confirmed: a mutex squatter's account name goes to standard error only, and `engine.log` never carries an account name (W4), 2026-10-28
- 13 (the residual risk in the build trees): accept: Task T3-25's desktop task run passes `-AcceptWritableBuild`, and the residual risk goes in these notes (Accepted in pull request 3, below), 2026-10-28; addendum: accept, `-AcceptWritableBuild` also accepts the writable parent `C:\lazymkv`, where a non-administrator could replace `C:\lazymkv\run` while no engine runs, and a protected parent folder waits for a later pull request (Ruling T3-24-c), 2026-10-29

- 15 (a `windows-latest` that is not elevated or has no credential vault): not needed: `windows-latest`'s token facts read `elevated=true type=Default integrity=S-1-16-12288 linked=false` (Task T3-2's CI run on pull request #14), its `win::creds` tests met no os error 1312, and the mini PC over SSH prints the same facts, 2026-10-28
